# Athletic Off-Season
_Your Blaze Camp plan_

> **Use at your own risk.** This plan is general fitness information, not medical advice. You follow it entirely at your own expense and risk, and you release Lalo Vera, Blaze Camp and BFit Studios Corp. from all liability. Stop and see a professional if something hurts. [Read the full disclaimer and release](https://github.com/Blazecamp/blaze-camp-model/blob/main/DISCLAIMER.md)

- **Main goal:** Athletic performance
- **Sport:** American football - tight end / linebacker (TE / LB, FB, box S) · Early off-season
- **Level:** Advanced (L3): 3 years of consistent training, training 4 days a week now
- **Plan type:** 12 weeks (recommended) · Week 1-12
- **Start date:** 11 Jan 2027
- **Training days per week:** 4 · Monday, Tuesday, Thursday, Friday · Session length (minutes): 90
- Use at your own risk, terms and release accepted: client, English
- Health check: answered; nothing in it asked for a doctor first. [Official PAR-Q+ (in English)](https://eparmedx.com/)

## Before you start

- Do the marked supersets on Monday, Tuesday, Thursday and Friday to save time.
- These days run over your 90 min even with the marked supersets: Monday in weeks 9-12 (about 93-99 min); Tuesday in weeks 11-12 (about 92-99 min); Friday in weeks 10-12 (about 91-98 min). Each of them shows what else saves time; or plan the extra minutes.
- Your sessions hold about 79% of your sport's weekly ankle-hop contacts: the work climbs across the block (not every week). Add a day or longer sessions for the full dose.
- Your sessions hold about 82% of your sport's weekly medball throws: the work still climbs every week. Add a day or longer sessions for the full dose.
- Your sessions hold about 72% of your sport's weekly change-of-direction cuts: the work climbs across the block (not every week). Add a day or longer sessions for the full dose.

## Your schedule

- Trained directly (10+ sets a week, climbing through the block): Quads, Hips (glutes + hamstrings), Back, Chest, Shoulders, Calves
- No direct exercise in this plan - they work inside your other lifts: Biceps, Triceps

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

_This plan starts at 3 in the tank and ends at 1 in the last week: the coach's "1 in the tank" is where you finish, so follow each week's number._

_In this plan your cardio sessions sit on lifting days: do each one as its own session that day, or move it to any free day._

Planned effort across the stairway: weeks 1-6: leave 3 in the tank; weeks 7-9: leave 2 in the tank; weeks 10-11: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 12: leave 1 in the tank. Week 12: one intensifier on each of these days (Monday, Tuesday, Thursday and Friday): a MYO set on the day's last small-muscle or machine exercise.

How heavy? Each exercise tells you how many reps to leave in the tank: "leave 2 in the tank" means you could do 2 more reps at the end of the set (about RPE 8; 3 left = RPE 7, 1 left = RPE 9). Your plan starts with 3 in the tank, then 2, then 1-2, and in the last week 1 with a few extra-hard sets. Week 1: start a little lighter, with a weight you could lift about 3 more times.

Olympic lifts are for power. Cleans, snatches and their dumbbell and kettlebell versions train power: move the weight as fast as you can with clean technique. Pick a weight you can move explosively on every rep; if it slows down or your technique breaks, it's too heavy. You don't need percentages for these unless you compete in Olympic weightlifting.

Core supersets are optional anywhere: you can pair a core exercise with any exercise to save time.

## How to adjust this plan

- Missed most of a week? Repeat that week before you move on. Missed one session? Do it on the next free day or let it go: never squeeze two sessions into one.
- A set felt like a true max (no rep left)? Next time take one set off that exercise, or a little weight.
- Everything feels easy? Add a little weight or a rep first; never jump ahead more than one week.
- Something hurts? First use a listed swap, then cut that exercise's sets by a third to a half, then drop it for the rest of the block. See a professional if it keeps hurting.
- Life got in the way for several weeks (sick, travel, busy)? Stop the block, take the recommended easy week if you want, then build a new block.
- You (or your coach) can switch off these blocks on any day: jumps, throws and speed; power / Olympic lifts; core; cardio; accessories. You can also add or remove exercises (more rear-delt work, for example). Use the suggested supersets to save time.
- Switching off the accessories block takes away every direct set for Calves.

Your plan climbs like a stairway: small steps up to week 6, a bit bigger from week 7, the hardest weeks at the end.

## Week 1 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Week level: 2 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**Baseline test this week (before you load up)** (Strength, Speed / agility, Jumps)

**Athletic work this week:** 63 jump contacts · 58 ankle-hop contacts · 57 throws · 57 direction changes · 120 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 33% · Receive-Create 1: Land

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 80 min_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Supersets marked today keep you inside 90 min._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** [A1] — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bird Dogs
   - Swap for: Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) / Hollow Rocks / High Plank (straight-arm plank) / Medball Plank Rockers
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Bird Dogs** [A2] — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Plank (forearm / low plank)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Dead Bugs / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) / March (anti-rotation marching) / Plank Supermans (held)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bird%20Dogs)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 6 x 10 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) / A-Skip (running drill) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations)
2. **Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)** [B1] — 3 × 7 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10 · SUPERSET W/ Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat / Medium Depth Drop / Low Depth Drop
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low%20Box%20Jump%20(Half-Shin%20Height))
3. **Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass** [B2] — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest · SUPERSET W/ Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg) / Medball Overhead Throw / Medball Chest Pass / Medball Squat to Chest Pass
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Sit-Up%20to%20Chest%20Pass)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull / KB Squat Clean / Hex-Bar Jump Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Then 2 back-off sets, about 3 reps in the tank.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
3. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Renegade Row / Bird Dog Row (DB) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
4. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Single-Arm DB Push Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
5. **Weighted Pull-Up** [C1] — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine)
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Wide-Grip Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet in Front / hollow) / Chin-Up (supinated) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up)
6. **Leg Extension (machine)** [C2] — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)

**Accessories**
1. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** [D1] — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB Y-Raise / Rear-Delt DB Row
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** [D2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

**Aerobic**
1. **Blue (A): short / high intensity · run** — 15 min · 30 s run / 30 s walk, repeat
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Blue%20(A)%3A%20short%20%2F%20high%20intensity%20%C2%B7%20run)

   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on

## Week 5 — Driving it

_Week level: 4.4 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: 16 exercises change a little (another version or one new detail); the rest stay
- Jumps move to Receive-Create 2: Land & Go
- +1 set on Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (Monday)

**Athletic work this week:** 99 jump contacts · 58 ankle-hop contacts · 69 throws · 63 direction changes · 300 m of sprints · 10 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 46% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Skip%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** [A1] — 2 × 25-45 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bird Dogs
   - Swap for: Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) / Hollow Rocks / High Plank (straight-arm plank) / Medball Plank Rockers
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Bird Dogs** [A2] — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Plank (forearm / low plank)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Dead Bugs / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) / March (anti-rotation marching) / Plank Supermans (held)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bird%20Dogs)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 5 x 15 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations)
2. **Depth Jump** [B1] — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest · SUPERSET W/ Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - First reactive jumps: two feet, low box, quality over height
   - Swap for: Depth Drop to Box Jump / Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps) / Tuck Jumps / Squirrel Jumps
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Depth%20Jump)
3. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** [B2] — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest · SUPERSET W/ Depth Jump
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Overhead Throw / Medball Chest Pass / Medball Squat to Chest Pass / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=i9M9ZnSFEw4)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull / KB Squat Clean / Hex-Bar Jump Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** [C1] — 3 × 7-9 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - Keep this lift at 7-9 reps.
   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** [C2] — 4 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Front Squat (barbell)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Renegade Row / Bird Dog Row (DB) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Single-Arm DB Push Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
4. **Weighted Pull-Up** [D1] — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Wide-Grip Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet in Front / hollow) / Chin-Up (supinated) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up)
5. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** [D2] — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)

**Accessories**
1. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** [E1] — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB Y-Raise / Rear-Delt DB Row
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** [E2] — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly
   - Then do its last 2 sets on their own
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

**Aerobic**
1. **Blue (A): short / high intensity · run** — 15 min · 40 s run / 20 s walk, repeat
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Blue%20(A)%3A%20short%20%2F%20high%20intensity%20%C2%B7%20run)

### Tuesday · B1 - single-leg hinge + push
_about 85 min_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Supersets marked today keep you inside 90 min._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Skip%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Side Plank (elbow)** [A1] — 2 × 25-45 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Dead Bugs
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (advanced: star side plank press) / Star Side Plank / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/at9CMT91HrM)
2. **Dead Bugs** [A2] — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Side Plank (elbow)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Bird Dogs / March (anti-rotation marching) / Plank Supermans (held) / Plank with Alternating Feet Raises
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/rbemelnkHag)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps)** — 3 × 9 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   > ADVANCED: taller hurdles (12 in and up) and continuous, quick-contact reps.
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Hurdle Hops (Forward-Lateral-Forward-Lateral) / Bilateral Lateral Hurdle Hops / Bilateral Forward Hurdle Hops / Tuck Jumps
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hurdle%20Hops%20(tall%20hurdles%2C%20continuous%20quick-contact%20reps))
2. **Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 15 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops / Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Backward%20Ankle%20Hops)
3. **Dig / Out Route** — 3 × 11 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) / Post / Corner Route / Slant Route / Flat Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dig%20%2F%20Out%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Snatch** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Snatch Pull / Single-Arm DB Snatch / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Low Hang (below the knee)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Snatch)

**Strength**
1. **Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)** [B1] — 5 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Incline Barbell Bench Press
   - per side = 1 set
   - Then do its last 2 sets on their own
   - Swap for: Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-on-Wall%20DB%20Deadlift%20(RDL))
2. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** [B2] — 3 × 7-9 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)
   - Keep this lift at 7-9 reps.
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press / Standing Cable Chest Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
3. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/9ZknEYboBOQ)

**Accessories**
1. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** [C1] — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Flat DB Fly
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Lying Leg Curl (machine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl)
2. **Flat DB Fly** [C2] — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Nordic Hamstring Curl
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Cable Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Fly)
3. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
4. **Machine Calf Raise - one leg at a time** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise%20-%20one%20leg%20at%20a%20time)

## Week 6 — Driving it

_Week level: 5 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press (Thursday)

**Athletic work this week:** 102 jump contacts · 60 ankle-hop contacts · 75 throws · 66 direction changes · 300 m of sprints · 10 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 46% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 84 min_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Supersets marked today keep you inside 90 min._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Skip (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** [A1] — 2 × 25-45 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bird Dogs
   - Swap for: Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) / Hollow Rocks / High Plank (straight-arm plank) / Medball Plank Rockers
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Bird Dogs** [A2] — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Plank (forearm / low plank)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Dead Bugs / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) / March (anti-rotation marching) / Plank Supermans (held)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bird%20Dogs)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 2 × 5 x 15 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations)
2. **Depth Jump** [B1] — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest · SUPERSET W/ Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - First reactive jumps: two feet, low box, quality over height
   - Swap for: Depth Drop to Box Jump / Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps) / Tuck Jumps / Squirrel Jumps
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Depth%20Jump)
3. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** [B2] — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest · SUPERSET W/ Depth Jump
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Overhead Throw / Medball Chest Pass / Medball Squat to Chest Pass / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=i9M9ZnSFEw4)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull / KB Squat Clean / Hex-Bar Jump Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** [C1] — 3 × 7-9 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - Keep this lift at 7-9 reps.
   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** [C2] — 4 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Front Squat (barbell)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Renegade Row / Bird Dog Row (DB) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Single-Arm DB Push Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
4. **Weighted Pull-Up** [D1] — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time
   - Keep this lift at 6-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Wide-Grip Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet in Front / hollow) / Chin-Up (supinated) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up)
5. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** [D2] — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)

**Accessories**
1. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** [E1] — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB Y-Raise / Rear-Delt DB Row
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** [E2] — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly
   - Then do its last 2 sets on their own
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

**Aerobic**
1. **Blue (A): short / high intensity · run** — 17 min · 40 s run / 20 s walk, repeat

   - Keep this lift at 7-9 reps.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press)

**Accessories**

   - Then do its last 2 sets on their own

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise%20-%20from%20a%20step%20(full%20stretch%20at%20the%20bottom))

## Week 7 — Driving it

_Week level: 6 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Step-Up (forward and lateral) (Tuesday)
- +1 set on Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL (Friday)

**Athletic work this week:** 102 jump contacts · 66 ankle-hop contacts · 78 throws · 66 direction changes · 360 m of sprints · 10 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 46% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

## Week 8 — Driving it

_Week level: 7 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Cable Fly (Friday)
- +1 set on Single-Arm Lat Pulldown (Thursday)

**Athletic work this week:** 102 jump contacts · 68 ankle-hop contacts · 81 throws · 69 direction changes · 360 m of sprints · 11 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 46% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

## Week 9 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 8 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: 15 exercises change a little (another version or one new detail); the rest stay
- Jumps move to Receive-Create 3: Repeat
- New bonus exercise: Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (Friday)
- +1 set on Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (Thursday)

**Athletic work this week:** 114 jump contacts · 68 ankle-hop contacts · 84 throws · 69 direction changes · 400 m of sprints · 11 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 48% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

   - Swap for: Plank Supermans (held) / Bird Dogs / March (anti-rotation marching) / Dead Bugs
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cross%20Connect%20(rolls%20%2F%20rocking%20%2F%20hold))

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Acceleration Starts** — 2 × 5 x 20 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) / A-Skip (running drill) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Acceleration%20Starts)
2. **DB Repeated Squat Jumps** [B1] — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10 · SUPERSET W/ Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams
   - 10-20% of body weight
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Broad Jump / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) / Single-Leg Broad Jump / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3kiH8WufBeg)
3. **Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams** [B2] — 3 × 9 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest · SUPERSET W/ DB Repeated Squat Jumps
   - 10-20 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) / Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Rotational%20Side-to-Side%20Floor%20Slams)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean & Jerk** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful

   - Swap for: Low-Hang Clean Pull / Power Clean / KB Squat Clean / Hex-Bar Jump Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**
1. **Stationary Lunge (Split Squat)** [C1] — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm Cable Row - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Walking Lunge / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/cnwphCgR60A)
2. **Single-Arm Cable Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** [C2] — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Stationary Lunge (Split Squat)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB/KB Row / Renegade Row / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) / Bird Dog Row (DB)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Cable%20Row%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)
3. **Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press / Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down) / Single-Arm DB Push Press
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORoOn93dnh4)
4. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown - 1-second squeeze at the top** [D1] — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine) - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - per side = 1 set
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XbZgoSNJXm4)

   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)

**Accessories**

   - Swap for: DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) / DB Upright Row / Seated DB Lateral Raise / Barbell Upright Row

2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top** [E2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ DB Lateral (Side) Raise - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - Then do its last set on its own

**Power / Olympic lifts**

**Strength**

**Accessories**

   - per side = 1 set
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20Leg%20Curl%20(machine))
3. **Cable Fly** [C2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seated Leg Curl (machine)
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Fly)
4. **Barbell Calf Raise - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)

## Week 12 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 11 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**End test this week: on the tested lifts, work up to one top set - it replaces that lift's top set - then do its back-off sets** (Strength, Speed / agility, Jumps)

_Last week: one intensifier on each of these days (Monday, Tuesday, Thursday and Friday): a MYO set on the day's last small-muscle or machine exercise_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Single-Arm DB/KB Row (Monday)
- +1 set on Machine Calf Raise (Tuesday)
- Shoulders: no new set this week (to fit your sessions) - add a little load or a rep

**Athletic work this week:** 66 jump contacts · 62 ankle-hop contacts · 57 throws · 66 direction changes · 120 m of sprints · 9 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 53% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
3. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 5 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Renegade Row / Bird Dog Row (DB) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
4. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Single-Arm DB Push Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
5. **Weighted Pull-Up - 1-second squeeze at the top** [C1] — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 2-3 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time
   - Swap for: Wide-Grip Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet in Front / hollow) / Chin-Up (supinated) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)
6. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** [C2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 2-3 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)

**Accessories**
1. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** [D1] — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB Y-Raise / Rear-Delt DB Row
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
2. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top** [D2] — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)

**Aerobic**
1. **Blue (A): short / high intensity · run** — 20 min · 40 s run / 20 s walk, repeat
   - Or use another of your cardio tools: bike. Go by time, not distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Blue%20(A)%3A%20short%20%2F%20high%20intensity%20%C2%B7%20run)

### Tuesday · B1 - single-leg hinge + push
_about 99 min_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Even with the supersets marked today this day runs over your 90 min: switch off a block, or plan the extra minutes._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Runs (high-knee running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) / A-Skip (running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Runs%20(high-knee%20running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Side Plank (elbow)** [A1] — 2 × 30-50 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Dead Bugs
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (advanced: star side plank press) / Star Side Plank / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/at9CMT91HrM)
2. **Dead Bugs** [A2] — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Side Plank (elbow)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Bird Dogs / March (anti-rotation marching) / Plank Supermans (held) / Plank with Alternating Feet Raises
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/rbemelnkHag)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Plate Hops** — 3 × 3 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Tuck Jumps / DB Ankle Hops / Single-Leg Squirrel Jumps / Squirrel Jumps
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Plate%20Hops)
2. **Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward)** — 2 × 10 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops / Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Right-Angle%20Ankle%20Hops%20(Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward))
3. **Comeback / Curl Route** — 3 × 10 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle / Dig / Out Route / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) / Post / Corner Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Comeback%20%2F%20Curl%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Snatch** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Snatch Pull / Single-Arm DB Snatch / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Low Hang (below the knee)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Snatch)

**Strength**
1. **Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)** — 5 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-on-Wall%20DB%20Deadlift%20(RDL))
2. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
3. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 2-3 min rest
   - Then 3 back-off sets, about 2 reps in the tank.
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press / Standing Cable Chest Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
4. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/9ZknEYboBOQ)

**Accessories**
1. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** [B1] — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Flat DB Fly
   - Keep this lift at 5-8 reps.
   - Swap for: Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Lying Leg Curl (machine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl)
2. **Flat DB Fly** [B2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Nordic Hamstring Curl
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Cable Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Fly)
3. **Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
4. **Machine Calf Raise - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)

   - Swap for: Plank Supermans (held) / Bird Dogs / March (anti-rotation marching) / Dead Bugs

   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)
3. **Test: countermovement jump** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 3 attempts, record the best.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams** — 3 × 5 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 10-20 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) / Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) / Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB/KB Row / Renegade Row / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) / Bird Dog Row (DB)

3. **Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Single-Arm DB Overhead Press / Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down) / Single-Arm DB Push Press

4. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown - 1-second squeeze at the top** [C1] — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 1-2 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Extension (machine) - 1-second squeeze at the top
   - per side = 1 set
   - Then do its last set on its own

   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box)

   - Then do its last set on its own

### Friday · B2 - deadlift + push
_about 98 min_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Even with the supersets marked today this day runs over your 90 min: switch off a block, or plan the extra minutes._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-Runs (high-knee running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-March (running drill) / A-Skip (running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-Runs%20(high-knee%20running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **High Plank (straight-arm plank)** [A1] — 2 × 30-50 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Side Plank Press (advanced: star side plank press)
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) / Hollow Rocks / Medball Plank Rockers
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=High%20Plank%20(straight-arm%20plank))
2. **Side Plank Press (advanced: star side plank press)** [A2] — 2 × 30-50 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest 30-60 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ High Plank (straight-arm plank)
   - Swap for: High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20Plank%20Press%20(advanced%3A%20star%20side%20plank%20press))

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Broad Jump** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: do the set in runs of 2-3 big jumps in a row (for 7 jumps: 3 + 2 + 2), resetting your arms between runs; full effort on every jump
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Broad Jump / DB Repeated Squat Jumps / Hex-Bar Jump Squat / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Broad%20Jump)
2. **Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops** — 2 × 21 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops / Bilateral Forward/Backward Ankle Hops / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Backward%20Ankle%20Hops)
3. **Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue)** — 3 × 12 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle / Comeback / Curl Route / Dig / Out Route / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Split-Step%20Reaction%20Drill%20(split-step%2C%20then%20shuffle%20%2F%20crossover%20%2F%20drop-step%20on%20a%20cue))

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Low-Hang Snatch Pull** — 3 × 4 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Snatch / Single-Arm DB Snatch / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) / Hang Power Snatch - from the High Hang
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low-Hang%20Snatch%20Pull)

**Strength**
1. **Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL** [B1] — 5 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 3-6 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Barbell Bench Press
   - Then do its last 2 sets on their own
   - Swap for: Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift / Snatch-Grip Barbell Deadlift / Sumo Deadlift / Wide-Grip Barbell Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Stiff-Leg%20Deadlift%20%2F%20RDL)
2. **Barbell Bench Press** [B2] — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 3-6 min after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press / Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press / Standing Cable Chest Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press)

**Accessories**
1. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (bonus)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Hold the dumbbells, take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift / Staggered-Stance Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB))
2. **Seated Leg Curl (machine)** [C1] — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Cable Fly
   - Then do its last set on its own
   - Swap for: Nordic Hamstring Curl / Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20Leg%20Curl%20(machine))
3. **Cable Fly** [C2] — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · rest 60-90 s after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seated Leg Curl (machine)
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Fly)
4. **Barbell Calf Raise - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise%20-%201-second%20squeeze%20at%20the%20top)
